    Σ_{q−1}(y·ψ_{q−1,i})·ξ_q^{i} = Σ_{q−1}(y)·ψ_{q−1,i}·ξ_q^{g_{q−1}}   for ARBITRARY y ∈ K_{q−1},

equivalently (take y = 1; Σ_{q−1} fixes 1) the **EIGEN-COEFFICIENT LAW**

    (EC-q)   Σ_{q−1}(ψ_{q−1,j}) = ξ_q^{g_{q−1}−j} · ψ_{q−1,j}    (j ∈ supp ψ_{q−1})

plus Σ_{q−1}-multiplicativity against ψ_{q−1,j} (Σ_{q−1} ∈ Aut(K_{q−1}),
supplied one level down by the same ladder). (EC-q) is exactly the
condition that the (Σ-DEF) twist preserve the relation ideal
(ψ_{q−1} ↦ ξ_q^{g_{q−1}}·ψ_{q−1}), making Σ_q a ring AUTOMORPHISM of
K_q — whence the shift commutes with Σ_q up to ξ_q^{E} for ALL E, and
(SC-q) closes.

## §4. Consequences: the δ-law and OPEN-2a-Σ unconditional

**COROLLARY SL-C1 (the Σ-LAW, map and sign pinned).** At every
shared-below junction at level m+1, every j ∈ supp:

    δ_j = ρ_j / Σ_m(ρ_j) .

*Proof.* δ_j = [Prnt_j(C_j)/R_mv(C_j)]/[Prnt_j(P_j)/R_mv(P_j)] (OA-L2's
invariant reading, consuming Thm-`phir` = OA-PHIR for the denominator);
by (Σ-LEDGER) both bracketed gauges are u_j·Σ_m(x)/x at x = the
respective reads, so δ_j = ρ_j·Σ_m(x/y) = ρ_j·Σ_m(ρ_j^{−1}·...)
— with x = R_mv(C_j), y = R_mv(P_j) = ρ_j·x: δ_j = (y/x)·Σ_m(x)/Σ_m(y).
Σ_m(x)/Σ_m(y): x and y = ρ_jx are the two reads
and the quotient identity δ_j = ρ_j/Σ_m(ρ_j) is
asserted as the ELEMENT identity that follows when Σ_m(y)/Σ_m(x) =
Σ_m(ρ_j) — this holds because **Σ_m ∈ Aut(K_m)**: the eigen-coefficient
law (EC-q) of §3's REMARK preserves the relation ideal at every
interior level 2 ≤ q ≤ m (ψ_{q−1} ↦ ξ_q^{g_{q−1}}·ψ_{q−1}), so the
composite Σ_m is a ring automorphism and Σ_m(y) = Σ_m(ρ_j)·Σ_m(x)
outright [r1, PE1 F1: re-pointed at the derived law. The sealed hedge —
"(SC)-type covariance when x is an ε-monomial-times-class
(x = ε_m(β_j)^{−1}ψ_{m,j}, REALIZE R3), by the same wrap-invariance as
§3's REMARK" — was INSUFFICIENT as stated: the step needs
Σ_m(ρ·x) = Σ_m(ρ)Σ_m(x) with ρ an ARBITRARY unit, i.e. the automorphism
property, i.e. ideal preservation = exactly (EC); OA-M1 never met this
because σ′ is coefficientwise-trivial below, while the composite Σ_m at

**COROLLARY SL-C2 (THEOREM OPEN-2a-Σ, now unconditional on its Σ-leg).**
At every shared-below junction at level m+1, m ≥ 1: strict ⟹ byte-equal;
hence recorded ξ = 1 ⟹ byte-equal, under EVERY selection rule. *Proof.*

## §5. The sign/map question RESOLVED (constructed separators)

All observed δ on the sealed battery were involutions, so the two boxed
variants ρ^{1∓σ′} could not be separated there (OPEN2ATTACK §4.3). The
resolution is by CONSTRUCTION (the constructed-counterexamples
discipline: derive where the candidate laws differ, then build it):

* **SEP-A (m = 2; the map is the inverse).** Chamber ℤ₃,
  (2,1,2)+(e₁,h₁,4): K₁ = F₉ with z₁ PRIMITIVE (ψ₀ = y²+y+2, ord 8);
  A₁ = 2, so ξ₂ = z₁^{−2e₁} has ORDER 4 yet is SUPPORT-INVISIBLE on
  ψ₁ = y⁴ + z₁^k (supp {0,4} ≡ 0 mod 4) — the m = 1 junction is
  byte-equal and the m = 2 junction is shared-below with a NON-involution
  twist. Monomial top moduli keep ρ in even z₂-parity (the W2-L2 parity
  collapse: texp + ε-exponent ≡ 0 mod ord — whence no separation on any
  monomial genre); NON-MONOMIAL top moduli (ψ₂ coefficient 1 + z₂ etc.)
  put both parities into the read and separate the maps. Measured
  (smoke, disclosed; sealed family SL-SEP2): on every such row δ is NOT
  an involution, δ_j = ρ_j/Σ₂(ρ_j) HOLDS, and BOTH bare-θ variants
  δ = ρ^{1∓σ′} FAIL.
* **SEP-B (m = 3; the χ-chain correction is real).** Chamber ℤ₃,
  (2,1,1)+(2,1,2)+(2,1,2) with ψ₂ = y² + (nonsquare of F₉): χ₂ =

Under the delegated end-to-end-proof criterion, the acceptance record is already the correct re-display. No further repair is required.

### 2. REAL — ξ₁ is undefined

Verbatim-ready append:

**[APPEND 2026-08-12 — base-character convention.]** Extend the ledger-character definitions in §1 by

    ξ₁ := 1.

This convention is forced by Σ₁ = id and makes the displayed base clause (SC-1) literal: for every y ∈ K₁ and every grid weight w,

    Σ₁(ε₁(w)·y)
      = ε₁(w)·y
      = ε₁(w)·ξ₁^{E(w)}·Σ₁(y),

because Σ₁ = id and ξ₁^{E(w)} = 1. Thus (SC-1) is the stated trivial base of the (VD-q)+(EC-q)+(SC-q) ladder. No higher-level definition or theorem statement changes.

### 5. REAL — SL-C1 has a dangling ellipsis

Verbatim-ready append:

**[APPEND 2026-08-12 — formal SL-C1 quotient calculation.]** In the proof of COROLLARY SL-C1, replace the dangling calculation beginning

    δ_j = ρ_j·Σ_m(x/y) = ρ_j·Σ_m(ρ_j^{−1}·...)

and its subsequent restart by the following single derivation.

Let

    x := R_m v(C_j),    y := R_m v(P_j).

By the definition of ρ_j used in OA-L2, y = ρ_jx, so ρ_j = y/x. By
(Σ-LEDGER),

    Prnt_j(C_j) = u_j·Σ_m(x),
    Prnt_j(P_j) = u_j·Σ_m(y).

Consequently,

    δ_j
      = [Prnt_j(C_j)/x] / [Prnt_j(P_j)/y]
      = [u_jΣ_m(x)/x] / [u_jΣ_m(y)/y]
      = (y/x)·Σ_m(x)/Σ_m(y).

The eigen-coefficient laws (EC-q) make Σ_m a ring automorphism; hence

    Σ_m(y) = Σ_m(ρ_jx) = Σ_m(ρ_j)·Σ_m(x).

Substitution gives, without any omitted term,

    δ_j = ρ_j / Σ_m(ρ_j).

This is exactly the displayed conclusion of SL-C1.

Consumer sweep: SL-C2 consumes only the unchanged identity `δ_j=ρ_j/Σ_m(ρ_j)` and injectivity of `Σ_m`; §5’s separator laws, §6’s `SL-DELTA` family, §7’s OPEN-2a conclusion, and §8’s theorem summary therefore survive verbatim.

artifacts.

**Provenance of the salvage:** the J-D dimension shortcut (character/orbit
counting of 𝒜(T)) was REFUTED with an explicit witness (WELD_SYNTHESIS §3.1:
dim is q-independent, counts are q-rational of unbounded data — already the
machine-checked order-0 instance separates them). J-D0 is the salvage that
survived: not "counts = dimensions" but "counts and dimensions are blind to
the same layer."

## S0. STATEMENT

Fix a decided OM shape (type skeleton) T of order m: the degree d0 of the
level-0 factor and the read triples (e_j, h_j, g_j), j = 0..m, together with
the residual-degree structure they induce — |K_j| = q^{d0·g_0···g_{j−1}}, the
residue fields where the level-j residual data live. The letters z_j (the
realized residual roots) and the carry cochain c (the [ILN]† value ledger) of
any tower REALIZING T are NOT part of the shape.

**The stratum count.** The count that L2core's per-cell coefficient consumes,
at each truncation window: the product over levels of

    L3_j  =  the residual-factorization count over F_{r_j}, r_j = |K_j|
             (the census of residual data of the prescribed degree/type
              at level j — the universal residue-shape polynomial genre)
    ×  the L4 lattice q-power  q^{±B}, B = the lattice exponent
             (the count of digit positions pinned/freed by the polygon
              side and its grid — integer position data).

**The carry gauge.** A 1-cochain u : γ ↦ u_γ ∈ K^* acting by anchor
rescaling a_γ ↦ u_γ·a_γ. Its full documented action:

    ledger:    c(γ,γ′)  ↦  c(γ,γ′) · u_γ u_{γ′} u_{γ+γ′}^{−1}   (= c·∂u)
    slot:      v        ↦  u·v          (unit character, per read slot)
    residual:  R(y)     ↦  ξ^{−deg R} · R(ξ·y)   (JA-RES geometric
               character; the (ξ,w) overall unit dies in monic form)
    positions, δ, D_j, grid abscissas:  UNCHANGED.

> **LEMMA J-D0.** (i) [GAUGE-INVARIANCE] For every decided shape T, every
> prime power q, and every 1-cochain u with unit values, the stratum count of
> T computed through the u-gauged read equals the direct stratum count.
> (ii) [VALUE-BLINDNESS] Any two towers/types with the same shape data
> (d0, (e_j, h_j, g_j)_j) and residual-degree structure have EQUAL stratum
> counts at every q, regardless of their letters and carry cochains.
> Consequence: the stratum count is a function of the SHAPE alone — per
> shape a single expression in q, the same at tame and wild primes.
>
> **GRADE: PROVED on the stated perimeter (unit-character gauges; the
> L3 × L4 assembly; honest boxes S4), consuming the supplier stack S7 at the
> grades listed there. Attempt 0/2.**

---

## S1. WHY THIS IS THE LEMMA THAT MATTERS (one paragraph)

The weld's hard open fences — (VD-m), (C-coll), (R-coll), (DMULT-s), the
one-perimeter character-triviality condition PERIM-μ of WELDMASTER §S3 — are
ALL value-layer fences: they condition on which unit characters are trivial.
J-D0 says count-layer statements never see that layer. So W-10 (the J-D2
count equality, the density bridge's counting leg) escapes the collapse
perimeter ENTIRELY: its hypotheses reduce to the proved/priced integer-grid
faces plus classical residual censuses (WELD_SYNTHESIS §3.2). That is the
single most consequential simplification the synthesis found for J-D.

## S2. PROOF

### S2.1 Leg L4 — positions are gauge-fixed and value-free

**(a) Units do not move valuations.** u_γ has w-value 0, and w is a
valuation on the graded layer (V1/V2 — the GD-2 spine, consumed at GRTJB's
ACCEPTED grade through W-3's development/polygon tie): w(u_γ·a_γ) = w(a_γ).
Every abscissa and ordinate the polygon, the grid, or the development
consumes is therefore unchanged by the gauge.
[machine: JD0-COB checks (1) and (3) — gauged anchors sit at their lines;
gauged anchor products sit at the sum line.]

**(b) The integer carry layer is split-only.** WM-COB(i):
D_j = t_j(β″_j) − t_j(β_j) − t_j(β′_j) = (∂t_j)(γ,γ′) — computed from the
eq-12 splits alone, integers in, integers out; likewise δ (the [ILN]† S0.3
outer count). No letter value enters. Citation note: WM-COB(i)'s footprint is
[ILN]† SHIFT-(n) + D_j-INT ONLY, both unconditional and ACCEPTED — the
WELDMASTER note that displays the identity is itself at arc 1/2, but this
clause rides the accepted [ILN]† layer, not the WM arc.
[machine: recorded per scored pair (`int_layer_sample`), not scored — the
split-only property is definitional in the engine.]

**(c) The lattice exponent is shape-only.** B is a function of grid
positions: the grid = T-side abscissas (JA-GRID — GRTJA, arc 1/2, priced),
the vertex-for-vertex polygon with slope/length/degree preserved (JB-VTX —
GRTJB, ACCEPTED 2/2), the same-division transfer (JB-DEV — GRTJB, ACCEPTED).
By (a) the gauge does not move these positions; by (b) the integer carry
data they generate are split-only. Hence B(T) is fixed by the shape and
unchanged under any unit gauge.

**(d) The gauge moves the ledger by exactly a coboundary.** With gauged
anchors a′_γ = u_γ·a_γ, the carry constant transforms as
c′(γ,γ′) = c(γ,γ′)·u_γ u_{γ′} u_{γ+γ′}^{−1} = (c·∂u)(γ,γ′): gauging moves
WITHIN the cohomology class; the class's integer layer is (b); the positions
are (a). So "carry gauge" is well-named: it is the coboundary action.
[machine: JD0-COB check (4), exact equality in K_top on every scored pair.]

### S2.2 Leg L3 — slot counts are gauge-fixed and value-free

**(e) The gauge acts on each slot as a unit character.** Through the
harness, the read of a u-gauged anchor is u·(the direct read) — a bijection
of the slot's value space K_j.
[machine: JD0-COB check (2); plus the fuzz leg (5): higher-w junk in the
representative changes neither position nor read — the read consumes only
the line.]

**(f) The induced action on residual polynomials preserves factorization
type POINTWISE.** The slot character acts on the level-j residual polynomial
by the variable rescaling σ_ξ : R(y) ↦ ξ^{−deg R}·R(ξ·y) (JA-RES's
z^{−iA}·C_i slot form; the overall unit (ξ,w) is killed by monic
normalization). σ_ξ extends the identity of K_j to a degree-preserving
K_j-algebra automorphism of K_j[y]; automorphisms carry factorizations to
factorizations, so deg, monicity, irreducibility, ψ(0) ≠ 0, and the full
factorization-degree multiset are preserved for EVERY R — not merely in
census. Consequently σ_ξ permutes the letter menu (monic irreducibles of
degree g_j with nonzero constant) and fixes every type census and the menu
cardinality.
[machine: JD0-L3 — full enumeration of every monic R of degree D ≤ 3 over
every roster level field (capped |K|^D ≤ 20000): pointwise type equality,
bijectivity of the gauged family, census equality, menu-count equality.]

**(g) Hence every L3_j count is gauge-invariant.** The stratum's residual
condition at level j is SHAPE data — a factorization-degree/type condition
on the slot value. By (f) the gauge preserves the condition pointwise, so
the direct and gauged strata are equal AS SETS slotwise; a fortiori the
counts agree. (More generally, any slot-wise bijection preserving the
condition preserves the count; the unit character does both. A bijection
NOT preserving the condition breaks counts — the NONCHAR tooth.)

### S2.3 Assembly and clause (ii)

Clause (i): the assembled count = ∏_j L3_j × q^{±B}; (c) fixes the exponent
leg, (g) fixes each census leg. ∎(i)

Clause (ii): let T, T′ have equal shape data. The assembly consumes ONLY:
positions and integer carry data — by (b)/(c) functions of the splits, i.e.
of (e_j, h_j)-data, equal for T, T′; and censuses over the K_j — the number
of monic polynomials of degree D with factorization type λ over F_r depends
only on r (the universal S_λ(q) polynomials; classical), and
r_j = q^{d0·g_0···g_{j−1}} is fixed by the residual-degree structure. The
letters z_j and the carry values c(γ,γ′) appear at NO point of the
assembly. ∎(ii)
[machine: JD0-BLIND — three constructed same-shape pairs whose letters
provably differ (different tuples; on two pairs even different
multiplicative orders): full stratum-count records equal across each pair,
including the realized polygon integer records (a genuine check that the
engine's positions do not leak values); the different-shape control pair
must differ — the SHAPE tooth.]

### S2.4 The algebra-level shadow (ACCEPTED input, displayed)

GRTJC W-9(iii) + JC-F1 [ACCEPTED 2/2]: at every composite stage the carry
algebra is the twisted group algebra K^c[ℤ/E] and

    𝒜(T) ≅ K[v]/(v^E − ζ_T),   dim_K 𝒜(T) = E   — independent of c.

Dimension is carry-blind; twisting by a coboundary gives an isomorphic
twisted algebra (K^{c·∂u}[ℤ/E] ≅ K^c[ℤ/E], basis rescale [γ] ↦ u_γ·[γ]).
And WM-COB(iii) [display owned by WELDMASTER, arc 1/2; value law on its
scored stratum]: the ENTIRE inner factor of the accepted cocycle is ∂E — a
coboundary of the ε-kit cochain. So at the algebra level the value layer
contributes nothing gauge-stable except the outer letter z̄^δ — and its
EXPONENT δ is integer/split data, already counted in the shape. J-D0 is the
count-level statement of the same death: what the gauge can move, counts
cannot see; what counts see (positions, δ, degree structure), the gauge
cannot move.

## S3. CONSEQUENCE — the uniformity-in-q mechanism at wild primes

Counts of decided strata are shape-only (S2.3), so per shape the count is
ONE expression in q — the L3 universal residue-shape polynomials times a
q-power with shape-determined exponent — valid at every residue
characteristic, wild included. Everything the wild carry layer could
contribute (letters, cochain values, the realization of its H² class)
enters the classifier only through the gauge orbit, and counts are constant
on gauge orbits.

This is the counting-side face of the interpretation note's H-B verdict
(HIGHLEVEL_INTERPRETATION_2026-08-07 §2, MEASURED): the wild part of the
carry class DIES over finite residue fields (all-wild classes trivial on
T3A/T2A; the tame ℤ/2 class survives on T2C). J-D0 prices both halves: the
wild death is harmless to densities because counts never consumed the class's
value realization in the first place; the tame survivor enters counts only
through the integer δ (position data), never through the letter z̄^δ.

And it is the lemma under H-C's positioning sentence (§3.2 there): **"Haar
volume is carry-gauge-invariant; the discriminant exponent is not."** The
corpus's own separator (HUMAN_PROOF §0: ∫|disc| = 7/12 ≠ 2/3 at p = 2 while
the volume is the uniform q/(q+1)) is exactly a functional that PRICES the
carries (v(disc) pays the wild different letter-by-letter), re-importing the
value layer that volume quotients out. The class of integrals the carry
algebra uniformizes = bounded functions factoring through the type map with
p-free weights — i.e. through the shape, i.e. through what J-D0 proves
gauge-blind. H-C supplied the vocabulary; this note supplies the lemma.

## S5. THE ORDER-0 LEAN TIE (displayed anchor, degenerate instance)

    density m σ q'  :=  ∑_{T ∈ omMenu m σ} omCount T q'

with `omCount : shape → ℕ → ℚ` — the evaluator's SIGNATURE takes the shape
and q′ only: no letter, no cochain, no tower argument exists to vary. So at
order 0 clause (ii) holds BY TYPE (definitionally), and clause (i) is empty
(E = 1, no carries). Displays: `omCount_sepShape_eq_rootCount` (the count as
a root census — the L3 leg at order 0), the non-vacuity gates

separation at q′ = 3 (`OM/RealInstanceV2Gates.lean`). The Lean tie is the
degenerate instance of J-D0 — an anchor for what the order-≥ 1 statement
must degenerate TO, not evidence for it.

### Dated adjudication + corrections (2026-08-12 — sol certification C10/E8: the as-built challenge adjudicated at HEAD)

The sol-5.6 as-built read of this note (gpt-5.6-sol high,
runs/cert/C10_jd0_output.log, 2026-08-12) returned CHALLENGE with 12
findings including a claimed CRITICAL. The E8 certification run
(gpt-5.6-sol high, runs/cert2/E8_jd0_output.log) adjudicated 6 REAL /
6 ARTIFACT — in particular the claimed CRITICAL (the "every
1-cochain" quantifier) is an ARTIFACT: S0 documents the quantified
cochain action as R(y) ↦ ξ^{−deg R}R(ξy), and the counterexample's
independent slot rescaling is outside that documented class. The six
REAL findings are repaired by the single S10 correction block below
(the full count-shape T̂ with Λ_j and 𝒢; the corrected LEMMA J-D0
display; degreewise multiplicativity replacing the automorphism
claim; the cohomology-class correction; classifier-identification
scope; consumer sweep). Everything above this heading is
byte-untouched by this append. The E8 adjudication, transcribed
unaltered:

The displayed theorem needs six repairs. The other six findings misread either the stated perimeter or the role of the battery/algebra shadow.

## Adjudication

1. **ARTIFACT.** The counterexample uses an independent diagonal rescaling of polynomial coefficients, which is not the note’s defined carry-gauge action. S0 explicitly specifies the action of the quantified cochain:

> “Its full documented action: … residual: \(R(y)\mapsto \xi^{-\deg R}R(\xi y)\).”

BOX-2 excludes “any value layer NOT of that form”; it does not narrow “every 1-cochain” to a subset after the fact. The warrant that the weld induces this action is priced at GRTJA 1/2, but that is supplier conditionality, not a quantifier contradiction.

2. **REAL.** The explicit definition of shape lists only \(d_0,(e_j,h_j,g_j)\) and the induced field sizes. It never includes the residual factorization predicate consumed by \(L3_j\). The phrase “prescribed degree/type” later in S0 cannot repair that omission.

3. **REAL.** The normalized degreewise map is not an algebra automorphism of \(K_j[y]\). Factorization preservation needs the degreewise multiplicativity argument supplied below.

4. **REAL.** “[C]omputed from the eq-12 splits alone” proves dependence on the chosen splits, not dependence only on \((e_j,h_j)\). No section-independence or canonical-section lemma appears at HEAD. The honest proved scope must retain the split/grid record as part of the count-shape.

5. **REAL.** S2.3 independently proves cochain-value blindness by non-occurrence of \(c\), but S3’s explanation incorrectly attributes the death of the entire class to constancy on gauge orbits. Gauge invariance alone only compares coboundary-related representatives.

6. **ARTIFACT.** S2.4 is explicitly called:

> “The algebra-level shadow”

and S7 expressly says:

> “S2.4 only, not load-bearing for S2.1–S2.3.”

The note does not use equality of dimensions to prove equality of counts or algebra isomorphism across distinct twisting classes.

7. **ARTIFACT.** The battery accurately says that its blind pairs have different letters; it does not say that they realize distinct cohomology classes. Clause (ii)’s carry-class independence is argued in S2.3 from:

> “the letters \(z_j\) and the carry values \(c(\gamma,\gamma')\) appear at NO point of the assembly.”

Thus this finding correctly describes test coverage but incorrectly treats that limitation as a proof gap.

8. **REAL.** BOX-4 cites exact verification through OM order 4 while the surrounding prose claims classifier identification “at all orders.” A finite verified range cannot supply the universal identification. The formal-product theorem remains universal; its transfer to the actual classifier must be limited to the supplier’s established perimeter.

9. **REAL.** “Factoring through the type map” is strictly weaker than being constant on fibers of `type → shape`. The uniformized integral class must be stated using the latter condition.

10. **ARTIFACT.** JD0-T-SHAPE concerns the particular registered pair `(C2A,C2B)`, whose records were expected to differ. The note never claims that the count function is injective on all shapes. Calling this one fixture a “different-shape control” does not assert the converse of shape-blindness.

11. **ARTIFACT.** S0 defines “stratum count” as the object consumed by the “per-cell coefficient,” including its \(q^{\pm B}\) factor. S6.1 then explicitly identifies \(q^{-3}\) as:

> “q-power \(q^{-3}\) in density normalization.”

Thus \((q-1)/q^3\) is not being presented as a raw finite-set cardinality. “Stratum count” is locally defined terminology for the normalized coefficient.

12. **ARTIFACT.** The finite scope is disclosed verbatim:

> “degree \(D\le3\) … capped \(|K|^D\le20000\)”

and:

> “37/37 towers, 68,523 checks.”

The universal argument is the symbolic proof in S2; the battery is recorded as supporting machine evidence. No sentence claims that the finite enumeration proves the universal quantifiers.

## Verbatim-ready repair append

This single append repairs findings 2, 3, 4, 5, 8, and 9.

## S10. CORRECTION (2026-08-12 — count-shape, degreewise residual action, and consumer scope)

This correction is controlling wherever the earlier wording says merely
“shape data,” calls the normalized residual map an algebra automorphism,
derives cross-class blindness from gauge-orbit constancy, identifies the
L3 × L4 assembly with the classifier at all orders, or equates factoring
through type with factoring through shape.

### S10.1 The full count-shape and corrected display

For each level j, let Λ_j denote the complete LETTER-FREE residual census
condition consumed by L3_j: its total polynomial degree, its full
factorization-degree multiset with multiplicities, and any nonzero-constant
or distinguished-factor DEGREE conditions. Λ_j contains no specified
residual root or coefficient value.

Let 𝒢 denote the complete discrete lattice datum consumed by L4: the chosen
eq-12 splits t_j, their induced integers

    D_j = t_j(β″_j) − t_j(β_j) − t_j(β′_j),

the corresponding δ-data, and the side/grid positions from which B is
computed. No assertion is made here that the chosen t_j, D_j, δ, or B are
determined by the shorter tuple (e_j,h_j)_j; proving such a reduction is a
separate grid/split theorem.

The COUNT-SHAPE is therefore

    T̂ = (d0, (e_j,h_j,g_j)_j, (Λ_j)_j, 𝒢).

Its residual fields still have

    r_j = |K_j| = q^(d0·g_0···g_{j−1}).

The letters z_j and all carry-cochain values c(γ,γ′) are not part of T̂.

For the normalization fixed by L2core, write

    μ_T̂(q) = q^(±B(𝒢)) · ∏_j N_{Λ_j}(r_j),

where N_{Λ_j}(r_j) is the finite-field census satisfying Λ_j. This μ_T̂(q)
is the “stratum count” or normalized per-cell coefficient used throughout
this note; N_{Λ_j}(r_j) is the raw residual cardinality.

> **LEMMA J-D0 (corrected display).**
> (i) [GAUGE-INVARIANCE] For every decided count-shape T̂, every prime
> power q, and every unit-valued carry gauge having the documented S0
> residual action
>
>     R(y) ↦ ξ^(−deg R) R(ξy),
>
> the normalized coefficient μ_T̂(q) computed through the gauged read
> equals the direct coefficient.
>
> (ii) [VALUE-BLINDNESS] Any two realized decided types with the same
> count-shape T̂ have equal normalized coefficients at every q, regardless
> of their residual letters and regardless of whether their carry
> cochains are cohomologous.
>
> Consequently μ_T̂(q) is a function of the full combinatorial count-shape
> T̂ alone.

Derivation of the repaired L4 clause: equality of 𝒢 fixes the chosen splits,
so the displayed formula D_j = ∂t_j gives equal D_j term by term; it fixes
the corresponding δ and grid positions as well. Hence it fixes B(𝒢).
Unit gauges do not move any of these positions because units have valuation
zero. This proves the L4 assertion at exactly the scope established by
S2.1, without inferring that an arbitrary change of section leaves D_j or
B unchanged.

Derivation of clause (ii): equality of T̂ gives the same fields K_j, the
same predicates Λ_j, and the same lattice exponent B(𝒢). Therefore every
factor N_{Λ_j}(r_j) and the q-power agree. The displayed formula for
μ_T̂(q) contains neither z_j nor c(γ,γ′), so equality holds even for carry
cochains in distinct cohomology classes. ∎

### S10.2 Correction to S2.2(f)

Delete the sentence

    “σ_ξ extends the identity of K_j to a degree-preserving
     K_j-algebra automorphism of K_j[y]”

and replace it by the following derivation.

For each fixed degree D define

    σ_{ξ,D}(R)(y) = ξ^(−D) R(ξy)

on the monic degree-D polynomials. It is a bijection with inverse
σ_{ξ^−1,D}. It is not asserted to be additive on polynomials of different
degrees. Instead, if R = AB with deg A = a, deg B = b, and D = a+b, then

    σ_{ξ,D}(R)
      = ξ^(−a−b) A(ξy)B(ξy)
      = σ_{ξ,a}(A) · σ_{ξ,b}(B).

Thus a factorization of R gives a factorization with exactly the same
factor degrees. Conversely, applying the degreewise inverse to a
factorization of σ_{ξ,D}(R) gives a factorization of R. Hence irreducibility
and the complete factorization-degree multiset are preserved. Monicity is
preserved because the leading coefficient is multiplied by ξ^(−D)ξ^D = 1,
and a constant term is nonzero exactly when its nonzero scalar multiple
ξ^(−D)R(0) is nonzero. This proves the pointwise preservation required by
S2.2(g).

### S10.3 Cohomology-class correction

Gauge invariance and cochain-value blindness have different proofs.
Clause (i) compares presentations inside one coboundary orbit:
c and c·∂u. It makes no assertion that distinct cohomology classes are
gauge-related. Clause (ii) compares distinct classes because the coefficient

    μ_T̂(q) = q^(±B(𝒢)) · ∏_j N_{Λ_j}(r_j)

contains no carry-cochain value at all. Accordingly, replace the S3 claim
that the entire value realization “enters the classifier only through the
gauge orbit” by:

    Unit gauges do not change the coefficient within a coboundary orbit;
    more strongly, the L3 × L4 formula is constant across different
    cohomology classes because no value of c occurs in that formula.

The algebra discussion in S2.4 remains only an algebra-level shadow and is
not proof of cross-class blindness.

### S10.4 Classifier-identification scope

J-D0 proves the preceding statements universally for the displayed formal
L3 × L4 coefficient. Its identification with L2core’s actual per-cell
classifier coefficient may be consumed only on the perimeter on which the
L2core supplier has established that identification. The cited exact
instances through OM order 4 do not, by themselves, prove an all-orders
identification.

Therefore “valid at every residue characteristic, wild included” is
unconditional for the formal coefficient μ_T̂(q). The same conclusion for
the actual classifier coefficient is unconditional through the supplier’s
proved/verified range and is conditional, at any further order, on a
general L2core assembly-identification theorem.

### S10.5 Corrected integral class

Let

    π : decided type → count-shape

forget the residual letters and carry-cochain values. A bounded function f
is uniformized by J-D0 precisely when it is constant on every fiber of π;
equivalently, when

    f = w ∘ π

for a weight w on count-shapes. Requiring the displayed rule for f to have
no explicit p-dependent coefficient is not sufficient: a rule can be
p-free while still distinguishing letters or carry values.

For f = w ∘ π with p-free w, grouping the integral by fibers gives

    ∫ f = ∑_T̂ w(T̂) μ_T̂(q),

so J-D0 supplies the carry-value blindness of the mass terms. This is the
correct scope of the H-C positioning sentence.

### S10.6 Consumer sweep

1. **W-10 at the (2,1,1) Eisenstein shape survives.** Its residual
   condition Λ, its eq-12 split/grid record 𝒢, and hence B are fixed in
   that row. The value-layer fences remain absent because neither its
   letter nor its carry-cochain values occur in μ_T̂(q). BOX-1’s outstanding
   grid/proviso obligations remain exactly as stated.

2. **W-11 survives with the corrected key.** The grown menu may be keyed by
   the full count-shape T̂. If W-11 wants to key it only by
   (d0,(e_j,h_j,g_j)_j), it separately owes proofs that Λ_j and 𝒢, or at
   least all their consumed censuses and B, are determined by that shorter
   tuple. No letter or carry-cochain value must be added to the key.

3. **W-12 / J-D1 survives.** It consumes the same coefficient equality,
   now at its exact proved scope.

4. **H-C survives with the correction in S10.5.** Its admissible weights
   are the p-free weights constant on fibers of type → count-shape.

5. **Fence bookkeeping survives.** PERIM-μ, (C-coll), (R-coll), and (VD-m)
   remain unnecessary for count-layer consumers whenever the consumer uses
   the L3 × L4 coefficient and the documented gauge dictionary. The repair
   does not promote the finite L2core checks into an all-orders
   assembly-identification theorem.
